            (@Ring.toSemiring.{0} Tiling.Plane
              (@SeminormedRing.toRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                (@SeminormedCommRing.toSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                  (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                    (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))))
            (@NormedAlgebra.toAlgebra.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Real.normedField
              (@SeminormedCommRing.toSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                  (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))
              (@RCLike.toNormedAlgebra.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instRCLike)))))
      r (Tiling.vertexSet s))
@Tiling.IsPolygonal.boundaryLocallyStraightAt_of_not_mem_vertexSet : ∀ {s : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane}
  (hs : Tiling.IsPolygonal s) {x : Tiling.Plane}
  (hx :
    @Membership.mem.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (@Set.instMembership.{0} Tiling.Plane)

        s)
      x)
  (hxv :
    Not
      (@Membership.mem.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (@Set.instMembership.{0} Tiling.Plane)
        (Tiling.vertexSet s) x)),
  Tiling.BoundaryLocallyStraightAt s x

@Tiling.exists_common_stabilizer_direction.{u_1} : ∀ {ι : Type u_1} (s : ι → Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
  (hne : ∀ (i : ι), @Set.Nonempty.{0} Tiling.Plane (s i))
  (hregular :
    ∀ (i : ι),
      @Eq.{1} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
        (@closure.{0} Tiling.Plane
          (@UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace.{0} Tiling.Plane
            (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Tiling.Plane
              (@SeminormedRing.toPseudoMetricSpace.{0} Tiling.Plane
                (@SeminormedCommRing.toSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                  (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                    (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField))))))
          (@interior.{0} Tiling.Plane
            (@UniformSpace.toTopologicalSpace.{0} Tiling.Plane
              (@PseudoMetricSpace.toUniformSpace.{0} Tiling.Plane
                (@SeminormedRing.toPseudoMetricSpace.{0} Tiling.Plane
                  (@SeminormedCommRing.toSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                    (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                      (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField))))))
            (s i)))
        (s i))
  (had :
    ∀ ⦃i j : ι⦄,
      @Ne.{u_1 + 1} ι i j →
        @MeasureTheory.AEDisjoint.{0} Tiling.Plane
